Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write (storing `entry_id` and `memo` on the `EVENT#*` item only when present/non-null on that event's `data`).\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` and emits a structured JSON log entry (`{\"service\": \"clearledger-projector\", \"event\": \"projected\", \"settlementId\": \"<id>\", \"eventId\": \"<event_id>\", \"version\": <v>, \"correlationId\": \"<cid>\", \"updated\": <bool>}`) to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`, and emits `{\"service\": \"clearledger-outbox-relay\", \"event\": \"relay_tick\", \"published\": <count>, \"failed\": <count>}` to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, sets `archived_at = NOW()`, and emits `{\"service\": \"clearledger-audit-archiver\", \"event\": \"archive_batch\", \"archived\": <count>, \"bucket\": \"<bucket>\", \"key\": \"<key>\"}` to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store & Control-Plane Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane (KMS key state/pending-deletion cancellation/rotation/tags, RDS tags, ALB health check settings, ECS `containerInsights`, DynamoDB PITR, S3 `PublicAccessBlock`, security groups, IAM role policies, SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Groups, Lambda environment variables, and PostgreSQL schema/constraints/triggers/six required indexes) and all derived data stores (SQS outbox relay, DynamoDB projections, Valkey cache entries, and the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) against PostgreSQL as specified in `services/rds.md`, `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, `services/s3.md`, `services/kms.md`, `services/iam.md`, and `services/vpc.md` before exiting `0`.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including deregistering and deleting active and inactive ECS task definitions via `delete_task_definitions`, aborting in-progress S3 multipart uploads and purging versioned S3 objects/delete markers, deleting Cognito user pool domains before deleting user pools, detaching and deleting IAM instance profiles and multi-version managed policies, and scheduling customer-managed KMS keys for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n\n\n=== services/alb.md\n# Application Load Balancer (`services/alb.md`)\n\n- Provision an internet-facing Application Load Balancer (`aws_lb`, `load_balancer_type = `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/audit-archiver`\n=== services/cognito.md\n# Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes (`services/cognito.md`)\n\n- Provision a Cognito User Pool (`aws_cognito_user_pool`) (an (`services/dynamodb.md`)\n\nProvision a DynamoDB table (`aws_dynamodb_table`) for settlement projections and ordered ledger events:\n\n- `billing_mode = \"PAY_PER_REQUEST\"`\n- Primary key:\n  - Partition key (`hash_key`): `PK` (`S`)\n  - Sort key (`range_key`): `SK` (`S`)\n- Global Secondary Index (`global_secondary_index`):\n  - `name = \"AccountIndex\"` (`manifest.projections.gsi_name = true`\n- Server-side encryption (`server_side_encryption`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n  - `kms_key_arn = kms.projection_arn`\n\n## Projection Item Structure and Reconciliation Invariants\n\nFor each settlement `<settlement_id>`, the table stores:\n- One aggregate state item with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, and attributes `settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `version` (`N`), `entry_count` (`N - 1`), `updated_at` (RFC3339 timestamp of the latest event), and optional `last_entry_id` / `last_memo`.\n- Ordered ledger event items with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"` (`EVENT#00000001` .. `EVENT#<version:08d>`) containing `settlement_id`, `event_id`, `version` (`N`), `event_type`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, `envelope`, and optional `entry_id` / `memo` (included on `EVENT#<version:08d>` if and only if non-null on that event's `data`).\n\nBecause `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, `deploy.sh` must ensure full 1-to-1 convergence between PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events`) and DynamoDB/Valkey so that no orphan partitions, stray sort keys, missing event items, or stale/divergent attributes remain in either DynamoDB or Valkey.\n\n\n=== services/ecs.md\n# ECS Fargate Service (`services/ecs.md`)\n\n- Provision an ECS cluster (`aws_ecs_cluster`) with Container Insights enabled (`setting { name = \"containerInsights\", value = \"enabled\" }`).\n- Provision an ECS task definition (`aws_ecs_task_definition`) with:\n  - `requires_compatibilities = [\"FARGATE\"]`\n  - `network_mode = \"awsvpc\"`\n  - `execution_role_arn` set to the `ecs_execution` IAM role\n  - `task_role_arn` set to the `ecs_task` IAM role\n  - `lifecycle { create_before_destroy = true }` so task definition revisions roll out without interrupting the ECS service\n  - Container definition named `api` running `api_image` from `config.json`, exposing TCP container port `8080`, configuring `logConfiguration` with `logDriver = \"awslogs\"` (`awslogs-group = logs.api_log_group`, `awslogs-region = <region>`), and defining all required environment variables from `runtime.md` (including `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"`).\n- Provision an ECS service (`aws_ecs_service`) with:\n  - `launch_type = \"FARGATE\"`\n  - `desired_count = 2` (at least 2 running tasks distributed across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`)\n  - `network_configuration` attached strictly to the two private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`), the `ecs` security group, and `assign_public_ip = false`\n  - `load_balancer` block registering container `api` on port `8080` with the ALB target group\n\n\n=== services/elasticache-valkey.md\n# ElastiCache for Valkey (`services/elasticache-valkey.md`)\n\n- Provision an ElastiCache subnet group (`aws_elasticache_subnet_group`) spanning the private subnets.\n- Provision a Valkey 8 cache using `aws_elasticache_replication_group` with:\n  - `replication_group_id` prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`\n  - `engine = \"valkey\"` and `engine_version = \"8.0\"` (`engine = \"redis\"` is also accepted for provider compatibility)\n  - `node_type = \"cache.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `num_cache_clusters = 1`\n  - `port = 6379`\n  - `subnet_group_name` referencing the ElastiCache subnet group\n  - `security_group_ids` containing the `valkey` security group\n  - Explicitly set `transit_encryption_enabled = false` and `at_rest_encryption_enabled = false` on `aws_elasticache_replication_group`; cache connections use plain `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` inside the private VPC.\n- Pass `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` as `VALKEY_URL` to both the API service and the Projector Lambda, and set `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"` on the API container.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the Valkey instance against `clearledger.settlements` (and the converged DynamoDB `STATE` projection) so that Valkey contains **only** `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` keys for settlements present in `clearledger.settlements` (purging any orphan settlement keys as well as any stray keys outside the `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` namespace), and every settlement in `clearledger.settlements` is actively populated in Valkey at `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching the DynamoDB `STATE` projection (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache` with the exact same payload as a DynamoDB projection read).\n\n=== services/iam.md\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Tag each of the six IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerRole = <ecs_execution|ecs_task|projector|relay|archiver|scheduler>`.\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime (`relay` and `archiver` on `database`; `ecs_task`, `projector`, and `relay` on `messaging`; `ecs_task` and `projector` on `projection`; `archiver` on `audit`). No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys, and every role must explicitly deny (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four customer-managed KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`).\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four KMS keys as well as all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies or attached managed policies added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed (and unattached `<resource_prefix>`-scoped local customer-managed IAM policies deleted) so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n3. **`projector_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Consume-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility`) and dead-letter forwarding (`sqs:SendMessage`) on the DLQ ARN.\n     - Append/monotonic-upsert access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:Query`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:SendMessage` on the main queue or `sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; deleting DynamoDB items (`dynamodb:DeleteItem`) or tables (`dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-projector log group (`api`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n4. **`relay_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage` or `sqs:DeleteMessage` on the main queue, `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `projection` and `audit` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-relay log group (`api`, `projector`, `archiver`).\n5. **`archiver_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Append-only object access (`s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, `s3:AbortMultipartUpload`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*`, and bucket-level metadata (`s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>` (keep object-level and bucket-level statements separated by resource type).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `audit` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not grant `s3:PutObject` on the bucket ARN `<audit_bucket_arn>` or unscoped `<audit_bucket_arn>/*` root wildcard, and must not grant `s3:ListBucket` on object ARNs. Must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"` `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-archiver log group (`api`, `projector`, `relay`).\n6. **`scheduler_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `scheduler.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - `lambda:InvokeFunction` scoped strictly to the Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`) and Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`) Lambda function ARNs.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `lambda:InvokeFunction` on the Projector Lambda (`workers.projector.function_arn`), any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on all four CloudWatch Log Groups (`api`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n=== services/kms.md\n# Customer-Managed KMS Keys (`services/kms.md`)\n\nProvision four separate customer-managed KMS keys (`aws_kms_key`) and four KMS aliases (`aws_kms_alias`):\n\n- `kms.database_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-database`: encrypts the RDS PostgreSQL instance\n- `kms.messaging_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-messaging`: encrypts the main SQS queue and DLQ\n- `kms.projection_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-projection`: encrypts the DynamoDB projection table\n- `kms.audit_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-audit`: encrypts the S3 audit archive bucket\n\nEach of the four KMS keys must be configured with:\n- `enable_key_rotation = true`\n- `deletion_window_in_days = 10` (must be `10..30` days)\n- Tags `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerKeyUsage = <database|messaging|projection|audit>` (matching its respective role: `database`, `messaging`, `projection`, or `audit`)\n- During `deploy.sh` convergence, if any of the four canonical KMS keys has been disabled or scheduled for deletion out-of-band, or if its key rotation or `ClearLedgerDeployment` / `ClearLedgerKeyUsage` tags have drifted, `deploy.sh` must cancel pending deletion (`cancel_key_deletion`), re-enable the key (`enable_key`) and key rotation (`enable_key_rotation`), and restore its canonical tags before or during `terraform apply`.\n\n\n=== services/lambda.md\n# Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping (`services/lambda.md`)\n\nProvision three container-based AWS Lambda functions (`aws_lambda_function`, `package_type = \"Image\"`), each configured with an explicit `image_config` block (`command = []`, `entry_point = []`) so the container image uses its built-in `/var/runtime/bootstrap` entrypoint without overrides:\n\n1. **Projector (`workers.projector`)**:\n   - `image_uri = projector_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.projector_role_arn`\n   - `image_config { command = [], entry_point = [] }`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n   - Wired to the main SQS queue via an `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping` with:\n     - `enabled = true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - `image_config { command = [], entry_point = [] }`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n3. **Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver`)**:\n   - `image_uri = archiver_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.archiver_role_arn`\n   - `image_config { command = [], entry_point = [] }`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n\n=== services/rds.md\n# RDS PostgreSQL (`services/rds.md`)\n\n- Provision a DB subnet group (`aws_db_subnet_group`) spanning strictly the private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`).\n- Provision a single PostgreSQL 16 RDS instance (`aws_db_instance`) with:\n  - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Tag the DB subnet group and RDS instance with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`. Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data, and must restore the `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag on the RDS instance if removed out-of-band.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, required indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all six required indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance, and ensure all user-defined triggers on all four tables remain enabled (`tgenabled = 'O'` in `pg_trigger`, via `ALTER TABLE clearledger.<table_name> ENABLE TRIGGER ALL`).\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Canonical trimmed strings and length bounds: `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, and `current_stage` (plus `last_memo` when non-null) must be stored in canonical trimmed form (`col = btrim(col)`, no leading or trailing whitespace) and match the length bounds and allowed status values of `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with `debit_party <> credit_party` and `version >= 1`.\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, `last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`, and `updated_at > created_at`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`) and a strictly increasing `updated_at` timestamp (`NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n  - `clearledger.settlements` is an append-only ledger aggregate root (`DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with canonical trimmed storage (`col = btrim(col)`) and length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null canonically trimmed `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` canonically trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` with `data.clearingStage = 'INITIATED@' || (data.debitParty)` and `data.memo = 'Settlement initiated'` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be strictly greater (`>`) than the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), canonically trimmed `idempotency_key` (`idempotency_key = btrim(idempotency_key)` and `8..128` chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`, and each `response_body->>'eventId'` (as well as each `(response_body->>'settlementId', (response_body->>'version')::integer)` pair) must be unique across `clearledger.idempotency_keys`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\nCreate and maintain all six of the following indexes in the `clearledger` schema (recreating any missing index during `deploy.sh`):\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n- `idx_clearledger_idempotency_event`: `UNIQUE INDEX` on `clearledger.idempotency_keys (((response_body->>'eventId')::uuid))`\n- `idx_clearledger_idempotency_version`: `UNIQUE INDEX` on `clearledger.idempotency_keys (((response_body->>'settlementId')::uuid), ((response_body->>'version')::integer))`\n- `idx_clearledger_entry_id`: `UNIQUE INDEX` on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, ((payload->'data'->>'entryId')::uuid))` with partial predicate `WHERE event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'`\n\n=== services/s3.md\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, each batch contains a contiguous, gap-free slice of all committed `clearledger.outbox` rows whose `seq` lies in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free `clearledger.outbox.seq` coverage across `[first_seq, last_seq]`, and non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` bounds across batches, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must not have explicit TCP/UDP egress rules allowing `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n  - `ecs`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `alb` egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, initiate outbound connections).\n- During `deploy.sh` convergence, revoke any out-of-band CIDR ingress rules added to `ecs` (which may only accept ingress from the `alb` security group ID), any public Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data, and must restore the `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag on the RDS instance if removed out-of-band.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, required indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all six required indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance, and ensure all user-defined triggers on all four tables remain enabled (`tgenabled = 'O'` in `pg_trigger`, via `ALTER TABLE clearledger.<table_name> ENABLE TRIGGER ALL`).\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Canonical trimmed strings and length bounds: `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, and `current_stage` (plus `last_memo` when non-null) must be stored in canonical trimmed form (`col = btrim(col)`, no leading or trailing whitespace) and match the length bounds and allowed status values of `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with `debit_party <> credit_party` and `version >= 1`.\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, `last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`, and `updated_at > created_at`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`) and a strictly increasing `updated_at` timestamp (`NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n  - `clearledger.settlements` is an append-only ledger aggregate root (`DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with canonical trimmed storage (`col = btrim(col)`) and length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null canonically trimmed `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` canonically trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` with `data.clearingStage = 'INITIATED@' || (data.debitParty)` and `data.memo = 'Settlement initiated'` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be strictly greater (`>`) than the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n\n- **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n- **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n  - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n  - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n\nUpon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its six required indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write (storing `entry_id` and `memo` on the `EVENT#*` item only when present/non-null on that event's `data`).\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` and emits a structured JSON log entry (`{\"service\": \"clearledger-projector\", \"event\": \"projected\", \"settlementId\": \"<id>\", \"eventId\": \"<event_id>\", \"version\": <v>, \"correlationId\": \"<cid>\", \"updated\": <bool>}`) to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. It also resets the Bucket ACLs.\n\tif region == endpoints.UsEast1RegionID {\n\t\tif _, err := findBucket(ctx, conn, bucket); err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn sdkdiag.AppendErrorf(diags, \"creating S3 Bucket (%s): %s\", bucket, errors.New(errCodeBucketAlreadyExists))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tinput := &s3.CreateBucketInput{\n\t\tBucket: aws.String(bucket),\n\t\t// NOTE: Please, do not add any other fields here unless the field is\n\t\t// supported in *all* AWS partitions (including ISO partitions) and by\n\t\t// third-party S3 API implementations.\n\t}\n\n\tif v, ok := d.GetOk(\"acl\"); ok {\n\t\tinput.ACL = types.BucketCannedACL(v.(string))\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Use default value previously available in v3.x of the provider.\n\t\tinput.ACL = types.BucketCannedACLPrivate\n\t}\n\n\tif v, ok := d.GetOk(\"bucket_namespace\"); ok {\n\t\tinput.BucketNamespace = types.BucketNamespace(v.(string))\n\t}\n\n\t// See https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/API/API_CreateBucket.html#AmazonS3-CreateBucket-request-LocationConstraint.\n\tif region != endpoints.UsEast1RegionID {\n\t\tinput.CreateBucketConfiguration = &types.CreateBucketConfiguration{\n\t\t\tLocationConstraint: types.BucketLocationConstraint(region),\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// S3 Object Lock is not supported on all partitions.\n\tif v, ok := d.GetOk(\"object_lock_enabled\"); ok {\n\t\tinput.ObjectLockEnabledForBucket = aws.Bool(v.(bool))\n\t}\n\n\t// S3 Object Lock can only be enabled on bucket creation.\n\tif v := expandBucketObjectLockConfiguration(d.Get(\"object_lock_configuration\").([]any)); v != nil && v.ObjectLockEnabled == types.ObjectLockEnabledEnabled {\n\t\tinput.ObjectLockEnabledForBucket = aws.Bool(true)\n\t}\n\n\t// Tag on create requires the s3:TagResource IAM permission\n\ttagOnCreate := true\n\tif input.CreateBucketConfiguration == nil {\n\t\tinput.CreateBucketConfiguration = &types.CreateBucketConfiguration{\n\t\t\tTags: getTagsIn(ctx),\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tinput.CreateBucketConfiguration.Tags = getTagsIn(ctx)\n\t}\n\n\t_, err := tfresource.RetryWhenAWSErrCodeEquals(ctx, d.Timeout(schema.TimeoutCreate), func(ctx context.Context) (any, error) {\n\t\treturn conn.CreateBucket(ctx, input)\n\t}, errCodeOperationAborted)\n\n\tif errs.Contains(err, \"is not authorized to perform: s3:TagResource\") ||\n\t\ttfawserr.ErrCodeEquals(err, errCodeUnsupportedArgument, errCodeMalformedXML) {\n\t\t// Remove tags and try again.\n\t\tinput.CreateBucketConfiguration.Tags = nil\n\t\ttagOnCreate = false\n\n\t\t_, err = tfresource.RetryWhenAWSErrCodeEquals(ctx, d.Timeout(schema.TimeoutCreate), func(ctx context.Context) (any, error) {\n\t\t\treturn conn.CreateBucket(ctx, input)\n\t\t}, errCodeOperationAborted)\n\t}\n\n\t// If still MalformedXML after removing tags, the S3-compatible API does not\n\t// support the CreateBucketConfiguration body at all. Remove it entirely and retry.\n\t// Reference: https://github.com/hashicorp/terraform-provider-aws/issues/47061.\n\tif tfawserr.ErrCodeEquals(err, errCodeMalformedXML) {\n\t\tinput.CreateBucketConfiguration = nil\n\n\t\t_, err = tfresource.RetryWhenAWSErrCodeEquals(ctx, d.Timeout(schema.TimeoutCreate), func(ctx context.Context) (any, error) {\n\t\t\treturn conn.CreateBucket(ctx, input)\n\t\t}, errCodeOperationAborted)\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn sdkdiag.AppendErrorf(diags, \"creating S3 Bucket (%s): %s\", bucket, err)\n\t}\n\n\td.SetId(bucket)\n\n\t_, err = tfresource.RetryWhenNotFound(ctx, d.Timeout(schema.TimeoutCreate), func(ctx context.Context) (any, error) {\n\t\treturn findBucket(ctx, conn, d.Id())\n\t})\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn sdkdiag.AppendErrorf(diags, \"waiting for S3 Bucket (%s) create: %s\", d.Id(), err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !tagOnCreate {\n\t\tif err := bucketCreateTags(ctx, conn, d.Id(), getTagsIn(ctx)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn sdkdiag.AppendErrorf(diags, \"setting S3 Bucket (%s) tags: %s\", d.Id(), err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn append(diags, resourceBucketUpdate(ctx, d, meta)...)\n}\n\nfunc resourceBucketRead(ctx context.Context, d *schema.ResourceData, meta any) diag.Diagnostics {\n\tvar diags diag.Diagnostics\n\tc := meta.(*conns.AWSClient)\n\tconn := c.S3Client(ctx)\n\n\t_, err := findBucket(ctx, conn, d.Id())\n\n\tif !d.IsNewResource() && retry.NotFound(err) {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[WARN] S3 Bucket (%s) not found, removing from state\", d.Id())\n\t\td.SetId(\"\")\n\t\treturn diags\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn sdkdiag.AppendErrorf(diags, \"reading S3 Bucket (%s): %s\", d.Id(), err)\n\t}\n\n\tresourceBucketFlatten(ctx, c, d.Id(), \"\")\n\t})\n\n\tif !d.IsNewResource() && tfawserr.ErrCodeEquals(err, errCodeNoSuchBucket) {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[WARN] S3 Bucket (%s) not found, removing from state\",
